00001         * MVS-6: SMARTER GHOSTS. COMMANDS U/D/L/R/Q, ONE PER CARD.
00002         * OPTIONAL FIRST CARD: AI<L> [<N>] : L = 0 STILL, 1 RANDOM,
00003         * 2 PERSISTENT DIRECTION, 3 CHASE (MANHATTAN); N = 1-3 GHOSTS.
00004         * GHOSTS NEVER REVERSE (LEVELS 2-3) UNLESS NO OTHER MOVE EXISTS.

00141          PROCEDURE DIVISION.
00142          MAIN-PARA.
00143              OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE.
00144              DISPLAY 'PACMAN MVS-6 SMART GHOSTS'.
00145              PERFORM READ-CMD.
00146              PERFORM READ-CONFIG THRU READ-CONFIG-EXIT.
00147              PERFORM INIT-GAME.
00148              MOVE 'START  ' TO WS-RESULT.
00149              PERFORM SHOW-STATE.
00150              PERFORM TURN-PARA
00151                  UNTIL WS-EOF = 1 OR WS-QUIT = 1 OR WS-WON = 1
00152                     OR WS-LOST = 1.
00153              IF WS-LOST = 1
00154                  DISPLAY 'LOST'
00155              ELSE IF WS-WON = 1
00156                  DISPLAY 'WIN'
00157              ELSE IF WS-QUIT = 1
00158                  DISPLAY 'QUIT'
00159              ELSE
00160                  DISPLAY 'EOF'.
00161              DISPLAY 'TURNS ' TICK-COUNT.
00162              DISPLAY 'SCORE ' SCORE.
00163              DISPLAY 'LIVES ' LIVES.
00164              CLOSE COMMAND-FILE.
00165              STOP RUN.
00166          READ-CONFIG.
00167              IF CMD-CHAR = 'A' AND WS-EOF = 0
00168                  PERFORM SET-LEVEL THRU SET-LEVEL-EXIT   4


00169                  PERFORM READ-CMD.
00170          READ-CONFIG-EXIT.
00171              EXIT.
00172          SET-LEVEL.
00173              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00174                  MOVE 0 TO AI-LEVEL
00175              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '2'
00176                  MOVE 2 TO AI-LEVEL
00177              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '3'
00178                  MOVE 3 TO AI-LEVEL
00179              ELSE
00180                  MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.
00181              IF CMD-COUNT = '2'
00182                  MOVE 2 TO GHOST-COUNT
00183              ELSE IF CMD-COUNT = '3'
00184                  MOVE 3 TO GHOST-COUNT
00185              ELSE
00186                  MOVE 1 TO GHOST-COUNT.
00187          SET-LEVEL-EXIT.
00188              EXIT.
00189          INIT-GAME.
00190              MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (PLAYER-Y PLAYER-X).
00191              PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00192                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00193              PERFORM CLEAR-GHOST-CELL VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00194                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00195              PERFORM COUNT-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00196                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00197          RESET-GHOST.
00198              MOVE GS-X (WS-G) TO GH-X (WS-G).
00199              MOVE GS-Y (WS-G) TO GH-Y (WS-G).
00200              MOVE GS-DIR (WS-G) TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00201          CLEAR-GHOST-CELL.
00202              MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO WS-GX.
00203              MOVE GH-Y (WS-G) TO WS-GY.
00204              MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (WS-GY WS-GX).
00205          COUNT-ROW.
00206              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00207                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00208          COUNT-CELL.
00209              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00210                  ADD 1 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00211          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00212              EXIT.
00213          TURN-PARA.
00214              PERFORM PROCESS-CMD THRU PROCESS-CMD-EXIT.
00215              PERFORM READ-CMD.
00216          READ-CMD.
00217              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 1 TO WS-EOF.
00218              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO WS-CMD.
00219          PROCESS-CMD.
00220              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00221              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00222              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO WS-RESULT.
00223              IF WS-CMD = 'U' OR WS-CMD = 'u'
00224                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00225                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE   5


00226              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'D' OR WS-CMD = 'd'
00227                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00228                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00229              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'L' OR WS-CMD = 'l'
00230                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X
00231                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00232              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'R' OR WS-CMD = 'r'
00233                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X
00234                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00235              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'Q' OR WS-CMD = 'q'
00236                  MOVE 1 TO WS-QUIT
00237                  MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE
00238              ELSE
00239                  DISPLAY 'INVALID COMMAND ' WS-CMD.
00240          PROCESS-CMD-EXIT.
00241              EXIT.
00242          TRY-MOVE.
00243              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00244              IF NEW-X < 1 OR NEW-X > 20 OR NEW-Y < 1 OR NEW-Y > 10
00245                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO WS-RESULT
00246              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (NEW-Y NEW-X) = '#'
00247                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO WS-RESULT
00248              ELSE
00249                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00250                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00251                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00252              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00253              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00254              PERFORM SHOW-STATE.
00255          ROUND-END.
00256              MOVE 0 TO WS-HIT.
00257              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00258              IF WS-HIT = 0 AND WS-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 0
00259                  PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00260                      VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00261                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00262                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00263              IF WS-HIT = 1
00264                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00265          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00266              EXIT.
00267          CHECK-HIT.
00268              PERFORM HIT-TEST THRU HIT-TEST-EXIT
00269                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00270          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00271              EXIT.
00272          HIT-TEST.
00273              IF GH-X (WS-G) = PLAYER-X AND GH-Y (WS-G) = PLAYER-Y
00274                  MOVE 1 TO WS-HIT.
00275          HIT-TEST-EXIT.
00276              EXIT.
00277          LOSE-LIFE.
00278              MOVE 0 TO WS-WON.
00279              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00280              IF LIVES = 0
00281                  MOVE 1 TO WS-LOST
00282                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO WS-RESULT   6


00283              ELSE
00284                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO WS-RESULT
00285                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00286                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00287                  PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00288                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00289                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00290                      MOVE 1 TO WS-WON.
00291          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00292              EXIT.
00293          SET-STATE.
00294              IF WS-LOST = 1
00295                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00296              ELSE IF WS-WON = 1
00297                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE
00298              ELSE
00299                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00300          SET-STATE-EXIT.
00301              EXIT.
00302          GHOST-MOVE.
00303              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00304              MOVE GH-DIR (WS-G) TO WS-CUR.
00305              MOVE REV-D (WS-CUR) TO WS-REVD.
00306              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00307                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00308              IF CAND-N = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 1
00309                  PERFORM TRY-REVERSE THRU TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00310              IF CAND-N > 0
00311                  PERFORM CHOOSE-DIR THRU CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT
00312                  PERFORM STEP-GHOST.
00313          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00314              EXIT.
00315          GHOST-PROBE.
00316              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00317              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00318              IF MAP-CELL (G-NY G-NX) NOT = '#'
00319                  IF AI-LEVEL = 1 OR WS-D NOT = WS-REVD
00320                      ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00321                      MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00322          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00323              EXIT.
00324          TRY-REVERSE.
00325              MOVE WS-REVD TO WS-D.
00326              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00327              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00328              IF MAP-CELL (G-NY G-NX) NOT = '#'
00329                  MOVE 1 TO CAND-N
00330                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (1).
00331          TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00332              EXIT.
00333          CHOOSE-DIR.
00334              IF AI-LEVEL = 3
00335                  PERFORM PICK-CHASE
00336              ELSE IF AI-LEVEL = 2 AND CAND-N = 1
00337                  MOVE CAND (1) TO WS-D
00338              ELSE
00339                  PERFORM PICK-RANDOM.   7


00340          CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT.
00341              EXIT.
00342          PICK-RANDOM.
00343              COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74.
00344              DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00345                  REMAINDER GHOST-SEED.
00346              DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00347                  REMAINDER WS-REM.
00348              ADD 1 TO WS-REM.
00349              MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D.
00350          PICK-CHASE.
00351              MOVE 999 TO BEST-DIST.
00352              PERFORM CHASE-EVAL THRU CHASE-EVAL-EXIT
00353                  VARYING WS-J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-J > CAND-N.
00354          CHASE-EVAL.
00355              MOVE CAND (WS-J) TO WS-K.
00356              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-K) - 1.
00357              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-K) - 1.
00358              COMPUTE DIST-X = G-NX - PLAYER-X.
00359              IF DIST-X < 0
00360                  COMPUTE DIST-X = 0 - DIST-X.
00361              COMPUTE DIST-Y = G-NY - PLAYER-Y.
00362              IF DIST-Y < 0
00363                  COMPUTE DIST-Y = 0 - DIST-Y.
00364              COMPUTE DIST-T = DIST-X + DIST-Y.
00365              IF DIST-T < BEST-DIST
00366                  MOVE DIST-T TO BEST-DIST
00367                  MOVE WS-K TO WS-D.
00368          CHASE-EVAL-EXIT.
00369              EXIT.
00370          STEP-GHOST.
00371              COMPUTE GH-X (WS-G) = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00372              COMPUTE GH-Y (WS-G) = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00373              MOVE WS-D TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00374          EAT-PELLET.
00375              IF MAP-CELL (PLAYER-Y PLAYER-X) = '.'
00376                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (PLAYER-Y PLAYER-X)
00377                  ADD 10 TO SCORE
00378                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00379              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00380                  MOVE 1 TO WS-WON.
00381          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00382              EXIT.
00383          SHOW-STATE.
00384              MOVE TICK-COUNT TO ST-TURN.
00385              IF WS-RESULT = 'START  '
00386                  MOVE ' ' TO ST-CMD
00387              ELSE
00388                  MOVE WS-CMD TO ST-CMD.
00389              MOVE WS-RESULT TO ST-RESULT.
00390              MOVE PLAYER-X TO ST-X.
00391              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO ST-Y.
00392              MOVE SCORE TO ST-SCORE.
00393              MOVE GH-X (1) TO ST-GX.
00394              MOVE GH-Y (1) TO ST-GY.
00395              MOVE LIVES TO ST-LIVES.
00396              MOVE GAME-STATE TO ST-STATE.   8


00397              MOVE PELLETS-LEFT TO ST-LEFT.
00398              DISPLAY WS-STATUS-LINE.
00399              IF GHOST-COUNT > 1
00400                  PERFORM SHOW-EXTRA THRU SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00401              PERFORM SHOW-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00402                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00403          SHOW-ROW.
00404              MOVE MAP-ROW (WS-ROW) TO WS-LINE.
00405              IF WS-ROW = PLAYER-Y
00406                  MOVE 'P' TO WS-LINE-CELL (PLAYER-X).
00407              PERFORM OVERLAY-GHOST THRU OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT
00408                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00409              DISPLAY WS-LINE.
00410          OVERLAY-GHOST.
00411              IF WS-ROW = GH-Y (WS-G)
00412                  MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO WS-GX
00413                  MOVE 'G' TO WS-LINE-CELL (WS-GX).
00414          OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT.
00415              EXIT.
00416          SHOW-EXTRA.
00417              MOVE GH-X (2) TO XL2-X.
00418              MOVE GH-Y (2) TO XL2-Y.
00419              IF GHOST-COUNT = 2
00420                  DISPLAY WS-XL2
00421              ELSE
